# DLS / TLN / CFE Errors — Block 4xx

This page lists every platform error message registered in numeric block 4xx. Messages come from the message catalogue; HTTP status, handler, and spec provenance come from the error catalog where recorded. Explanation, likely cause, and recovery are curated for documented blocks and shown as — elsewhere.

## DLS — Deals

### `401_DLS_COUNTERPARTY_CLIENT_RELATIONSHIP_INVALID`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `401_DLS_COUNTERPARTY_CLIENT_RELATIONSHIP_INVALID` |
| HTTP status | 422 |
| Message | The selected counterparty does not have a valid active relationship (Bank, FSP Trader, Supplier, or Customer) with the client. Please select a different counterparty. |
| Explanation | The counterparty chosen for the deal has no valid active relationship with the client, so the deal cannot be confirmed. |
| Likely cause | The counterparty is not linked to the client as a Bank, FSP Trader, Supplier, or Customer, or that relationship is expired or inactive. |
| Recovery | Select a counterparty that holds an active relationship with the client, or ask an administrator to set up the relationship on the business partner records. |
| Source | srv/reuse-functions/validateCounterpartyClientRelationship.ts · spec 001 · DLS conductPreConfirmCheck |

### `402_DLS_COUNTERPARTY_NOT_FOUND`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `402_DLS_COUNTERPARTY_NOT_FOUND` |
| HTTP status | 422 |
| Message | A counterparty must be selected for this deal. |
| Explanation | Every deal must name a counterparty, and this one does not. |
| Likely cause | The counterparty field was left empty, or the selected record was deleted before confirmation. |
| Recovery | Choose a counterparty from the value help list and save the deal again. |
| Source | srv/reuse-functions/validateCounterpartyClientRelationship.ts · spec 001 · DLS conductPreConfirmCheck |

### `403_DLS_CLIENT_NOT_FOUND`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `403_DLS_CLIENT_NOT_FOUND` |
| HTTP status | 422 |
| Message | The selected client does not exist. |
| Explanation | The client referenced on the deal does not exist in the system. |
| Likely cause | The client record was deleted, or the deal references an invalid or stale client identifier. |
| Recovery | Re-select the client from the value help list. If the client is missing, ask an administrator to create or restore the business partner record. |
| Source | srv/reuse-functions/validateCounterpartyClientRelationship.ts · spec 001 · DLS conductPreConfirmCheck |

| HTTP status | — |
| Message | The user who captured a deal cannot confirm it (segregation of duties). |
| Explanation | — |
| Likely cause | — |
| Recovery | — |
| Source | — |

### `404_DLS_CLIENT_MUST_BE_BUSINESS_PARTNER`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `404_DLS_CLIENT_MUST_BE_BUSINESS_PARTNER` |
| HTTP status | 422 |
| Message | The selected business partner is not a client. |
| Explanation | The business partner selected as the client is not classified as a client. |
| Likely cause | The partner exists but does not carry the client category, so it cannot be on whose behalf a deal is traded. |
| Recovery | Select a business partner that is flagged as a client, or have an administrator assign the client category to the partner. |
| Source | srv/reuse-functions/validateCounterpartyClientRelationship.ts · spec 001 · DLS conductPreConfirmCheck |

### `405_DLS_SOD_CAPTURE_CONFIRM_SAME_USER`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `405_DLS_SOD_CAPTURE_CONFIRM_SAME_USER` |
| HTTP status | 403 |
| Message | You cannot confirm a deal that you captured. A different user must perform the confirmation. |
| Explanation | Segregation of duties blocks the confirmation: the user who captured the deal cannot also confirm it. |
| Likely cause | The same person performed the capture and is attempting the confirm step, which governance rule SoD-001 forbids. |
| Recovery | Ask a different user with the dealer or manager role to open the deal and confirm it. |
| Source | srv/deals/utils.ts · spec 002 · DLS confirmDeal |

### `406_DLS_DEAL_NOT_FOUND`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `406_DLS_DEAL_NOT_FOUND` |
| HTTP status | 404 |
| Message | The specified deal could not be found. |
| Explanation | The deal the action is being performed on could not be found. |
| Likely cause | The deal was deleted, or the page is showing a stale link to a deal that no longer exists. |
| Recovery | Refresh the list and open the deal again. If the deal genuinely does not exist, verify the deal number with the originator. |
| Source | srv/deals/utils.ts · spec 002 · DLS confirmDeal, cancelDeal |

### `407_DLS_DEAL_NOT_IN_CAPTURED_STATUS`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `407_DLS_DEAL_NOT_IN_CAPTURED_STATUS` |
| HTTP status | 409 |
| Message | This deal is not in CAPTURED status. Only captured deals can be confirmed. |
| Explanation | Only deals in captured status can be confirmed, and this deal is in a different status. |
| Likely cause | The deal was already confirmed, cancelled, or otherwise moved on by another action. |
| Recovery | Refresh the deal and check its lifecycle status. If it is already confirmed there is nothing further to do; if it was cancelled, capture a new deal. |
| Source | srv/deals/utils.ts · spec 002 · DLS confirmDeal |

### `408_DLS_DEAL_ALREADY_TERMINAL`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `408_DLS_DEAL_ALREADY_TERMINAL` |
| HTTP status | 409 |
| Message | This deal has already reached a terminal status and cannot be cancelled. |
| Explanation | The deal has reached a terminal status such as settled, matured, or cancelled, so it cannot be cancelled again. |
| Likely cause | A cancel action was attempted on a deal whose lifecycle is already complete. |
| Recovery | No cancellation is possible. If the settled position must be unwound, use the reversal or termination process instead of cancel. |
| Source | srv/deals/utils.ts · spec 002 · DLS cancelDeal |

### `409_DLS_CONTRACTUAL_TERMS_LOCKED`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `409_DLS_CONTRACTUAL_TERMS_LOCKED` |
| HTTP status | 409 |
| Message | The contractual terms of this deal are locked after confirmation. Field(s) affected: {1}. |
| Explanation | The contractual terms of a confirmed deal are locked and cannot be edited; the message lists the fields that were touched. |
| Likely cause | An update changed one or more locked contractual fields after the deal was confirmed. |
| Recovery | Edit only the fields that remain open after confirmation. To change locked terms, cancel before confirmation or use rollover or termination after confirmation. |
| Source | srv/deals/validation.ts · spec 002 · DLS before UPDATE |

### `410_DLS_NO_CATEGORIES_CONFIGURED`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `410_DLS_NO_CATEGORIES_CONFIGURED` |
| HTTP status | 422 |
| Message | No accounting event categories are configured for this deal type and occasion. |
| Explanation | The confirmation cannot complete because no accounting event categories are configured for this deal type and occasion. |
| Likely cause | The accounting definition for the instrument or occasion has not been set up for the company. |
| Recovery | Ask a system administrator to configure the accounting event categories for this deal type and occasion, then confirm the deal again. |
| Source | srv/deals/utils.ts · spec 002 · DLS confirmDeal |

### `411_DLS_CATEGORY_PROCESSOR_NOT_FOUND`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `411_DLS_CATEGORY_PROCESSOR_NOT_FOUND` |
| HTTP status | 422 |
| Message | No accounting processor is registered for the category '{1}'. |
| Explanation | An accounting event category was resolved for the deal, but no processor is registered to handle it. |
| Likely cause | The category is configured but the matching accounting processor is missing or misconfigured. |
| Recovery | Contact a system administrator to register or correct the processor for the category, then retry the confirmation. |
| Source | srv/deals/utils.ts · spec 002 · DLS confirmDeal |

### `412_DLS_CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `412_DLS_CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION` |
| HTTP status | 409 |
| Message | This deal was modified by another user. Please refresh and try again. |
| Explanation | Another user changed the deal while this action was in progress, so the change was rejected to protect against overwriting. |
| Likely cause | Two users had the same deal open and saved conflicting changes. |
| Recovery | Refresh the deal to load the latest version, reapply the intended change, and try again. |
| Source | srv/deals/utils.ts · spec 002 · DLS confirmDeal |

### `413_DLS_GL_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `413_DLS_GL_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND` |
| HTTP status | 500 |
| Message | The general ledger account could not be found. |
| Explanation | The posting pipeline could not find the general ledger account needed for this deal. |
| Likely cause | The account mapping for the financial node is missing, or the referenced account was removed. |
| Recovery | Ask an administrator to configure or restore the general ledger account mapping, then retry the confirmation. |
| Source | srv/deals/utils.ts · spec 002 · DLS executePostingPipeline |

### `414_DLS_GL_ACCOUNT_CLASSIFICATION_MISSING`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `414_DLS_GL_ACCOUNT_CLASSIFICATION_MISSING` |
| HTTP status | 500 |
| Message | The general ledger account has no classification assigned. |
| Explanation | The general ledger account found for the posting has no classification assigned, so the pipeline cannot determine how to post it. |
| Likely cause | The account was created without a general ledger account classification. |
| Recovery | Ask an administrator to assign a classification to the account, then retry the confirmation. |
| Source | srv/deals/utils.ts · spec 002 · DLS executePostingPipeline |

### `415_DLS_ACTING_CAPACITY_INVALID`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `415_DLS_ACTING_CAPACITY_INVALID` |
| HTTP status | — |
| Message | Invalid acting capacity {1}. Must be OWN_BOOK, ON_BEHALF, or AGENCY. |
| Explanation | The acting capacity recorded on the deal is not one of the allowed values. |
| Likely cause | The field was set to a value outside own book, on behalf, or agency — for example through an import with a typo. |
| Recovery | Set the acting capacity to one of the three valid values and save again. |
| Source | — |

### `415_DLS_NO_CLOSING_LEGS`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `415_DLS_NO_CLOSING_LEGS` |
| HTTP status | 422 |
| Message | At least one closing leg is required for a rollover proposal. |
| Explanation | A rollover proposal must close at least one leg of the existing position, and none were provided. |
| Likely cause | The rollover was proposed without selecting any closing legs. |
| Recovery | Add at least one closing leg to the rollover proposal and submit again. |
| Source | srv/reuse-functions/rolloverValidation.ts · spec 003 · DLS proposeRollover |

### `416_DLS_NO_OPENING_LEGS`

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `416_DLS_NO_OPENING_LEGS` |
| HTTP status | 422 |
| Message | At least one opening leg is required for a rollover proposal. |
| Explanation | A rollover proposal must open at least one new leg, and none were provided. |
| Likely cause | The rollover was proposed without defining any opening legs. |
